The `public contributions` by users in Madagascar on `2026/10/23 1:52 AM UTC`. This list contains users from `Madagascar` and cities `Antananarivo` `Toamasina` `Mahajanga` `Antsirabe`.

There are `138 countries` and `675 cities` can be found [here](https://github.com/xiv3r/top-github-users-ranking).

There are `970 users`  in Madagascar. You need at least `4 followers` to be on this list.

🏅 Looking for a shareable ranking badge? Visit the [Madagascar GitHub user ranking page](https://github.com/gayanvoice/top-github-users-ranking/blob/main/markdown/madagascar.md) to view the country rankings and copy the ready-made badge snippet for your GitHub profile or README.
